Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biogeocenology operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 563. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 38 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the biogeocenology decomposition. By minimality, the element a^563 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 38-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
